(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "fin"
              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
              (hash
                (hash "fin"
                  (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
            (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "caps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (y rndx) (x expt))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "fin"
              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
              (hash
                (hash "fin"
                  (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
            (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "caps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false)))))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr request response text) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr text) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
            (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "y" z-0 y))))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "fin"
              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
              (hash
                (hash "fin"
                  (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
            (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "caps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))))
  (label 0)
  (unrealized (0 1))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
        (y y) (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0)))

(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "fin"
              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
              (hash
                (hash "fin"
                  (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
            (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "caps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (y rndx) (x expt))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "fin"
              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
              (hash
                (hash "fin"
                  (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
            (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "caps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false)))))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (sr response cr request text) (cpk spk akey) (s c ca name)
    (y rndx) (x expt))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x))
  (deflistener response)
  (non-orig (invk cpk))
  (uniq-orig sr response)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall ((sr text) (response text) (cpk akey) (y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z 5) (p "" z-0 2) (p "server" "sr" z sr)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "server" "response" z response) (p "" "x" z-0 response)
          (non (invk cpk)) (ugen y) (uniq-at sr z 1)
          (uniq-at response z 4)) (false))))
  (traces
    ((recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))

(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fin"
                (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))

                (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk

        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash
            (hash "fin"
              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))

              (cat
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc s spk (privk ca)))

                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))

                (hash "fin"

                  (hash cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                    (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
            (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "caps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)

          (hash "saps" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))))
  (label 62)
